Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [71] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CAGE 框架，用于认证工具使用型 LLM 智能体在面临绑定错误和数值漂移时的动作授权安全性，解决了分类通道和数值通道分别认证无法组合的问题，通过精确枚举离散分支并认证连续扰动，在多种场景下消除了误放行，同时保持了有用的自主决策比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运行时权限门控仅基于观测到的工具返回值授权动作，无法防范返回值与其来源绑定时的微小错误（如分类字段绑定错误、数值漂移）。论文旨在解决：在声明的合理绑定返回邻域内（一个可接受的绑定错误加上有界数值漂移），候选动作是否仍保持授权。

Method: 提出 CAGE 认证框架，直接认证分类和数值通道的联合邻域：精确枚举离散分支（分类通道的绑定错误），并在每个分支内认证连续扰动（数值漂移）。提供三种变体：CAGE-Exact 用于可执行策略的精确认证；CAGE-Lip 和 CAGE-RS 在策略不可执行时，基于显式测量的保真度假设认证学习到的门控。

Result: 在合成、策略即代码、监管合规和真实交易四种设置中，CAGE 消除了精确逐点门控所承认的预算内误放行，同时保持了有用比例的自主决策。CAGE-Exact 实现精确认证，CAGE-Lip 和 CAGE-RS 在保真度假设下提供认证保证。

Conclusion: 分类和数值通道的分别认证不可组合，联合认证是必要的。CAGE 通过精确离散枚举与连续认证相结合，为工具使用型 LLM 智能体提供了实用的鲁棒授权机制，在保证安全性的前提下维持了决策自主性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [72] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MirrorCraft 基准，通过成对的 Vanilla 与 Mirror 世界（仅服务端规则不同）评估 LLM 智能体在隐藏规则变化下的泛化能力。实验显示规则变化对不同任务套件影响差异大，ReAct 在无规则描述时表现最佳，提供精确规则仅带来适度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Minecraft 基准多在固定游戏机制下评估 LLM 智能体，无法反映智能体在配方、掉落等核心规则发生隐性变化时的持续进步能力。需要一种受控的成对评测环境，隔离规则变化因素，量化智能体的适应性。

Method: 构建 MirrorCraft：每个 Mirror 世界复制对应的 Vanilla 世界，仅通过数据包修改服务端规则（配方、掉落等），地形、出生点、资源分布、目标、接口、动作预算保持一致。包含 5 个受控生物群系、6 套规则变更、3 个进程目标、2 个模型家族、6 种智能体配置，统一使用 Mineflayer 接口。采用确定性进阶里程碑与成功率评估，引入规则干预效应（RIE）度量 Vanilla 与 Mirror 世界的性能差异。

Result: 隐藏规则变化在不同规则套件上影响显著不同；在无规则描述条件下，ReAct 配置获得最高的汇总 Mirror 得分；提供精确规则描述仅在三个目标上带来适度的平均进度与完成率提升。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft 将 Minecraft 评测扩展至非固定机制场景，提供了一个受控环境，用于研究智能体如何利用游戏玩法结果在当前世界规则与先验知识不符时进行适应。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [73] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PRISM，一种基于策略空间分解与组合的多奖励强化学习框架，通过优化独立的正向策略和全局负向策略，缓解多目标冲突，实现推理时的可控偏好组合，在科学推理、工具使用推理及有益性-安全性对齐任务上超越基线并提供额外的推理时偏好控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型需适应多样人类价值与使用场景，多奖励强化学习虽重要，但面临严重的对齐税问题：不同优化目标相互权衡甚至冲突，导致后训练不稳定、低效。

Method: PRISM 不直接组合奖励，而是分解策略空间：训练一组独立的正向策略（各对应单一奖励）和一个全局负向策略（捕捉共同不良行为），推理时通过灵活组合这些策略实现可控的偏好调节。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理、有益性-安全性对齐三类任务上，PRISM 持续优于现有多奖励 RL 基线，并展现出推理时偏好控制的额外可控性。

Conclusion: 策略空间分解与组合是解决多奖励 RL 冲突的有效路径，PRISM 通过分离正向与负向策略优化，同时实现了训练稳定性提升与推理时偏好灵活控制，为大模型多目标对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [74] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文识别出模式格式的工具规范是大语言模型作为代理部署时安全性下降的主要原因，提出SafeKeep推理时防护机制，通过解耦安全判断与工具执行（使用扁平化文本规范进行安全评估，保留原始模式规范用于执行），显著提高有害请求拒绝率并降低提示注入攻击成功率，且保持任务处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在作为代理部署时安全性大幅下降，但降级原因尚不明确。本文旨在找出根本原因并提供有效的推理时防护方案。

Method: 通过白盒表征分析发现模式格式工具规范削弱模型内部拒绝信号，导致不安全工具执行。基于此提出SafeKeep：推理时将安全判断与工具执行解耦，安全评估使用扁平化文本工具规范，执行阶段保留原始模式格式规范。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个大语言模型（含白盒和黑盒）上，SafeKeep将有害请求平均拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，将观察级提示注入攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%，优于现有防护方法并保持任务处理能力。

Conclusion: 模式格式工具规范是代理安全性下降的关键因素；SafeKeep通过解耦安全判断与执行有效缓解该问题，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [75] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGA方法，通过根据结构化动作重新分配训练信号，解决跨环境GUI代理中专家模型融合导致的动作冲突和蒸馏信号均等对待的问题，在两个模型规模上均达到最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的GUI代理通常针对特定领域（移动、Web、桌面），限制了部署和用户体验；权重融合会因专家分歧破坏可执行动作，而在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽避免了冲突教师监督，但将所有响应令牌同等对待，忽略了动作令牌是环境与代理交互的唯一接口。

Method: MAGA根据生成动作的正确性重新分配训练信号：抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，聚焦于错误动作的学习；引入仅训练时的提示（training-only hint），在不改变学生输入的情况下优化领域专家教师提供的监督信号。

Result: 在两个模型规模（8B等）上，MAGA达到最高平均成功率，比最强基线高出2.0%（在8B规模），并与教师模型达到几乎相同的平均性能。

Conclusion: MAGA通过动作感知的训练信号重分配和仅训练时提示，有效整合了跨环境专家模型，显著提升了统一GUI代理的性能，接近教师模型水平。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了257篇关于智能体AI系统验证的论文，提出五维度分类法（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体），指出行为评估较成熟，但时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性、开放式多智能体保障仍欠发达，并给出三个安全关键领域案例研究，最后提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI系统通过多步轨迹结合规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应来行动，传统的组件测试和单次输入输出评估已不足，因为可接受行为取决于决策随时间和在变化环境条件下的展开。因此需要系统性地表征智能体系统的验证问题。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，综合257篇涵盖智能体评估、软件保障、网络物理系统、运行时监控和监管指导的论文，构建五维度分类法（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体），并以此映射现有方法并揭示覆盖缺口；辅以三个跨域案例研究（医疗、工业操作、智能交通）说明分类法在安全关键场景中的复现。

Result: 分析显示行为评估相对成熟，而时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可读性和开放式多智能体系统保障仍处于欠发达状态。案例研究基于文献中记录的失败模式，展示了五个分类维度在安全关键设置中的反复出现。

Conclusion: 可信部署智能体AI取决于在上下文中验证轨迹，而非仅评估孤立组件。论文提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程，聚焦有界自主规范、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和可审计证据结构，以推动智能体系统验证的进一步发展。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [77] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ModelEquivBench，一个多关系评估系统，用于认证大语言模型生成的优化模型与真值之间的语义等价性，提供E0-E6七层语义档案及可独立验证的证据。评估三个模型快照发现，粗粒度基线无法捕捉的细微差别：许多可执行候选在至少一个关系上被认证为负例，且结构拒绝发生在E2认证可行集相等的情况下。三个模型在不同阶段失效，无法用单一准确率衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将生成模型与真值简化为单一等价/不等价判决或执行成功率，这些标签既无法独立验证，也不忠实反映两个公式在多种不同意义上的一致性。

Method: 提出ModelEquivBench，一个认证的多关系评估系统，报告每对模型的语义档案E0-E6：模型构建与精确摄入(E0)、验证表示对齐(E1)、同空间与投影可行集关系(E2,E3)、目标序等价(E4)、最优值相等(E5)、最优解集等价(E6)。每个判定条目附带关系适当的可独立重检证据：E0-E1的可重放轨迹或显式映射，E2-E6正面结论的精确有理数证书，支持的负面结论的显式反例。未完成映射搜索、不支持结构、资源限制产生类型化的UNKNOWN或N/A而非猜测，未满足前提报告为ABSENT。

Result: 在冻结的173个基础问题（每模型346个单元）上评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B三个模型快照，采用无修复协议。结果显示：49、35、25个单元包含可执行候选但至少在一个支持关系上被认证为负例；25、8、18个结构拒绝发生在E2认证映射可行集相等的配对上。三个模型在不同档案阶段失效，无法有意义地归约为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: ModelEquivBench提供了细粒度、可认证的多关系评估，揭示了大语言模型生成优化模型时的多样化失效模式，证明了单一标量指标不足以捕捉模型间的语义等价质量差异。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives. Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出分析型记忆作为检索型记忆的补充，设计AdaMM框架联合支持两种记忆，通过从多模态交互中抽取溯源属性值观测、发现重复字段结构并物化供分析访问，在MemEye和MemGallery基准上分别提升11.3%和7.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统主要关注检索型记忆，通过摘要和索引组织交互历史以返回查询相关信息，但缺乏对跨交互累积观测进行过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等分析计算的能力，因此需要互补的分析型记忆抽象。

Method: 提出AdaMM框架：1) 从对话、图像和上下文元数据中抽取带溯源的属性值观测；2) 自动发现重复出现的字段结构并物化为可查询的分析结构；3) 推理时采用记忆感知规划器将查询分解为检索操作和分析操作，分别路由至相应工具执行。

Result: 在两个长期多模态记忆基准MemEye和MemGallery上实验，AdaMM分别将性能提升高达11.3%和7.3%，验证了联合检索与分析记忆的有效性。

Conclusion: 分析型记忆是长期多模态记忆系统的必要补充，AdaMM通过自动模式发现和物化实现了无需应用预定义模式的灵活分析访问，显著提升了复杂查询下的记忆利用效果。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [79] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SESA（自进化技能增强智能体），通过将程序化记忆作为工具增强搜索自我博弈的演化状态，实现任务生成与技能记忆的双向协同进化。实验表明，SESA 在七个开放域和多跳问答基准上显著超越 SSP 和 SkillRL，且演化后的技能记忆既能内化为模型参数，又可作为外部记忆在推理时调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我博弈智能体虽能生成训练问题，但其课程缺乏持久状态：失败仅影响梯度，不显式塑造未来练习。外部技能记忆虽保留程序经验，却通常基于固定任务分布学习。需要一种机制使技能记忆与任务生成共同演化，并使技能收益同时内化进模型参数和外部记忆库。

Method: SESA 引入挑战者和求解器分离的自我博弈框架：挑战者生成问题，求解器检索外部技能记忆并尝试解决。信息量大的失败被蒸馏为可复用技能并写回记忆。更新后的记忆改变求解器行为和成功率，进而改变挑战者奖励和未来问题分布，形成双向演化循环。检索到的技能塑造在策略训练轨迹，使收益既进入模型参数又保留在外部记忆库。

Result: 在七个开放域和多跳问答基准上，SESA 平均准确率比 SSP 提高 1.2–3.2 分，比技能增强基线 SkillRL 高 0.9 分。在 Qwen3 模型上，SESA-Off（无推理时检索）仍保持比 SSP 高 1.8–2.2 分，最终技能库再额外贡献 0.5–1.0 分。

Conclusion: 演化技能记忆不仅是推理时插件，更能改变策略学习和未来训练分布，同时保持作为可选外部记忆的价值，实现了记忆自由部署和推理时可选检索的双重优势。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [80] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMTFV框架，通过数学工具流(MTF)解耦验证建模与执行，利用数学工具箱实现精确计算，在多个数据集和模型上显著提升验证准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型数学推理验证方法存在不足：自然语言反思难以支持精确计算，直接生成验证程序过早耦合建模与底层实现，导致验证不可靠。

Method: AMTFV引入数学工具流(MTF)作为中断-执行-恢复接口，验证智能体构建验证工作流，将数学对象和计算意图编码为MTF请求发送给数学工具箱智能体，后者解析请求、生成可执行调用并分发至后端进行精确计算，工具输出支持候选答案裁决、答案修正和验证工作流修正。

Result: 在五个数学推理数据集和七种模型配置上实验，AMTFV整体优于代表性基线；单模型配置下平均准确率比最强基线提升高达8.3个百分点，在中高验证复杂度样本上增益更大。

Conclusion: AMTFV通过解耦验证建模与具体执行，引入数学工具箱实现精确计算，有效提升了大语言模型数学推理答案验证的可靠性和准确性，尤其在复杂验证任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [81] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COntExt框架，利用结构化运营指标定义自动扩展本体，通过父类预测、关系类型预测、数据属性分配三个子任务，在四个网络安全本体上评估，证明指标衍生上下文优于本体上下文基线，表明运营指标目录是本体扩展的实用且未被充分利用的来源，可显著降低本体维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地以结构化、机器可读格式定义运营指标以监控系统、流程和合规性，这些指标隐含领域知识（概念、属性、关系），往往超出正式本体捕获的范围，但指标目录与本体知识的关联仍依赖人工、临时且劳动密集型方法，亟需自动化框架利用指标上下文扩展本体。

Method: 提出COntExt框架，输入结构化指标定义，利用指标上下文建议如何将引用的概念和属性集成到现有本体。将扩展问题分解为三个子任务：父类预测、关系类型预测、数据属性分配。在四个网络安全本体上评估不同算法，对比指标衍生上下文与本体上下文基线。

Result: 实验结果显示，指标衍生上下文在关系类型预测和数据属性分配任务上优于本体上下文基线；父类预测任务上表现相当。证明运营指标目录是本体扩展的实用且未被充分利用的来源。

Conclusion: COntExt框架展示了如何利用结构化运营指标自动扩展本体，显著降低人工本体工程成本，为组织维护本体提供低成本解决方案，推动本体与运营指标的深度融合。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [82] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMUR框架：通过多人的偏好反馈交互式学习多目标强化学习策略，无需预定义奖励函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策任务常涉及多竞争目标（如性能与效率），真实奖励函数难以指定。现有MORL假设每个目标有明确奖励函数，继承单目标RL难题；PbRL虽能从人类反馈学习奖励，但主要研究单目标设置。两者存在gap。

Method: 提出LEMUR (Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback)，代理交互式地从多人的偏好中学习，联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，使代理在学习过程中有效平衡竞争目标。

Result: 在多种基准多目标任务上评估，实验结果显示LEMUR优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为无预定义奖励函数解决多目标决策任务提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [83] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DungeonBench 是一个基于《龙与地下城》战斗系统的战术推理基准测试，包含单次遭遇战和多日冒险两种轨道，评估模型在复杂规则、资源管理、几何位置、时机把握等多维度的决策能力。实验表明，前沿语言模型虽能赢得单次战斗，但在跨天资源预算、休息时机和规则感知战术纪律上仍显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏和模拟器基准测试多忽略规则密集的战术推理，即在几何、时机、资源、目标和规则交互同时起作用时做出最优选择的能力。作者旨在构建一个保留完整 D&D 5e 战斗机制（基于 2014 SRD）且可由模拟器精确解析的基准，填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 DungeonBench 模拟器引擎，覆盖绝大多数战斗相关的 2014 SRD 内容，每一步提供完整战术观测、待决策和可执行选项索引列表（移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标、准备、稀缺资源）。设置两条评估轨道：Encounter（单次遭遇战局部战术）和 Day（跨遭遇战持久生命值、法术位、消耗品、准备和短休时机）。同一决策流支持启发式控制器、语言模型策略、学习型选项排序器和掩码动作强化学习智能体。

Result: 在共享决策流上评估前沿语言模型策略：完整战术观测未使基准饱和；模型常能赢得直接遭遇战，但在关联的多日冒险中暴露出资源预算失当、休息时机把握不佳、缺乏规则感知的战术纪律等系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: DungeonBench 成功揭示了当前前沿语言模型在复杂、规则密集、长时程战术推理中的短板，特别是跨遭遇战的资源管理和规则遵从性。该基准为未来研究提供了一个标准化、可扩展且机制完备的测试床，推动战术推理能力的系统性提升。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [84] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务的顺序基准，用于评估LLM代理在超参数优化中的迭代实验能力。测试了12个主流代理，发现它们具备可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代优化、复杂日志诊断和稳定接近参考性能方面存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从代码补全系统演变为自主科学代理，评估其进行实验的能力变得至关重要。现有基准主要关注静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性，缺乏对代理解读实验证据并以此指导后续超参数决策能力的直接评估。

Method: 构建AgentHPOBench顺序基准，包含跨越七大研究类别的30个可执行ML任务。每个任务从验证过的基线运行开始，代理执行多轮顺序干预。每一步代理观察累积的配置、指标和日志，再提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估12个主流代理和传统HPO基线。

Result: 当前代理在多个领域展现出可测量的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代优化、复杂日志诊断、以及稳定向报告的参考性能推进三个方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: AgentHPOBench填补了评估自主科学代理实验推理能力的空白。虽然代理显示出潜力，但在持续迭代优化和诊断推理方面仍有显著局限，指明了基于代理的实验科学需进一步发展的方向。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [85] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向变风量（VAV）暖通空调系统的故障检测与诊断本体（FDD-ON），旨在解决FDD领域数据可解释性与互操作性不足的问题，通过模块化、可扩展的本体形式化表示系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及属性，建立因果-故障-症状-影响显式关系，为诊断知识查询、异构FDD输出映射及互操作应用开发提供机器可解释的语义基础。实验基于公开VAV数据集验证，结果表明FDD-ON为可扩展、透明、互操作的FDD解决方案奠定了基础语义框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FDD技术在建筑暖通空调系统中的有效部署受限于领域知识结构化缺失，导致异构数据源、多样设备类型和诊断输出难以融合。数据可解释性与互操作性不足形成信息孤岛，阻碍了数字孪生驱动的FDD框架和AI驱动的维护决策系统等高级应用的实施。

Method: 构建模块化、可扩展的FDD本体（FDD-ON），形式化表示VAV暖通空调系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性；整合HVAC FDD语义，提供受控词汇支持的故障、症状、影响属性综合表示；建立故障、症状、影响库覆盖广泛运行异常及后果；定义显式的贡献原因-故障-症状-影响关系，作为机器可解释的知识基础。

Result: 基于公开VAV暖通空调数据集评估并通过FDD开发应用演示，FDD-ON能有效支撑诊断知识查询、异构FDD输出映射及互操作应用开发，为跨应用的可扩展、透明、互操作FDD解决方案提供了基础语义框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON通过本体化建模解决了FDD领域的语义互操作难题，实现了故障因果链的显式表示，为数字孪生、AI维护决策等上层应用提供了标准化的语义支撑，推动了建筑暖通空调系统故障诊断向智能化、集成化方向发展。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [86] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ExtractBench基准，评估模式引导抽取的价值准确性、记录完整性、溯源性和成本，发现商业VLM在短文档表现好但长文档截断，编码代理准确但成本高，LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在三项指标均排名第一，准确性媲美编码代理但成本低得多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流越来越依赖代理进行模式引导抽取，但缺乏综合评估基准来同时衡量价值准确性、大规模记录完整性、溯源性和成本。

Method: 构建ExtractBench基准，包含4869页、370个企业文档、8个业务领域、67种文档类型，带有挑战场景标签；采用可扩展的模式和真值构建流水线，结合独立系统一致性（真实文档）、已知值（合成列表）和人工验证（表单）；使用顺序无关的价值F1评估价值准确性，词级和页级F1评估溯源性。

Result: 商业VLM在短文档表现良好，但在长文档上常截断记录列表；编码代理保持较高准确性但成本高得多；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在价值准确性、溯源性（词级和页级F1）三项指标均排名第一，准确性与编码代理相当，成本仅为其很小一部分。

Conclusion: ExtractBench填补了模式引导抽取综合评估的空白，揭示了不同方法在长文档处理上的权衡，并证明了LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在准确性和成本效益上的优势。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [87] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据账本裁决流程，将每个声明与证据包配对并分配支持关系，将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明退回作者。在2335条盲测基准上，Agent证据账本条件达成0.676关系准确率和0.601宏F1，显著优于最佳非Agent基线（0.383/0.303），并能有效路由矛盾/缺失/混合证据声明，实现AI辅助写作的可审计溯源层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理起草声明的速度快于作者核实引用或检索证据是否支持的速度，需要一种可追踪的工作流来确保声明与证据的对应关系，并自动识别不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明以便退回作者修正。

Method: 构建基于AVeriTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER和SciFact的2335行盲测基准，隐藏金标准关系和证据标签仅用于评分。提出证据账本裁决工作流：为每个声明配对证据包，分配支持关系（支持/矛盾/缺失/混合），并将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明路由回作者。

Result: Agent证据账本条件达到0.676关系准确率和0.601宏F1，优于最佳非Agent基线（0.383准确率，0.303宏F1）。成功路由1270/1435个金标准为矛盾、缺失证据或混合证据的声明，同时仅路由295/900个支持型声明。

Conclusion: 证据账本裁决能将异构证据包转化为AI辅助写作的可审计溯源层，有效提升声明-证据可追踪性和质量控制。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


### [88] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模型大语言模型自动同行评审的基准测试协议，用于评估自主AI科学家系统生成论文的质量。实验表明，FARS基准论文在四个核心维度上显著优于其他四个领先框架，且Gemini和Claude评审结果高度一致，验证了自动化评估的可靠性，但GPT-5.4的评价标准与其他两者差异较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI科学家系统的发展，如何客观、可扩展地评估其生成论文的质量成为亟待解决的问题。现有缺乏统一的量化基准和可靠的自动化评估方法，因此本文旨在建立首个针对AI科学家系统的定量基准，并验证多模型LLM评审的一致性与有效性。

Method: 采用一致的15个研究提案，运行四个AI科学家框架生成60篇论文，另加15篇FARS基准论文共75篇。使用三个独立LLM评审器对论文在原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度、重要性四个维度进行1-5分打分，计算平均分并通过Spearman相关系数分析评审器间一致性。

Result: FARS基准论文平均分2.14-2.47，显著高于其他系统的1.00-1.87，在Gemini和Claude评审中领先优势超过2倍。Gemini与Claude评分高度相关（ρ=0.907，p<0.001），且均与综合分极强相关（ρ=0.961，p<0.001），而GPT-5.4与其他两者相关性较弱（ρ≈0.32），提示其评价准则不同。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI科学家系统的定量基准，证明多模型LLM自动评审能提供可扩展、一致的自主研究质量评估框架。结果揭示了不同LLM评审器可能采用不同评价标准，未来需进一步统一评价维度以提高评估可靠性。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [89] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了自我验证细化（SVR），一种无需外部 Oracle 的多轮强化学习框架，通过模型自身的正确性判决和置信度评分来自适应控制推理时的计算预算，在数学推理任务上以更少的轮次超越了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么统一分配测试时计算预算导致在简单输入上浪费算力，要么依赖外部验证器引导细化，需要额外反馈。需要一种能够自主判断何时停止、何时继续细化的内部控制机制，且不依赖推理时的真实标签或外部 Oracle。

Method: SVR 在每一轮让模型同时输出解答、离散的正确性判决和置信度分数；仅当判决为 Correct 且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则继续自我细化。训练使用 GRPO 在固定轨迹长度上进行，奖励函数包含解答正确性、校准感知的自我验证、以及停止就绪的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理时激活，真实标签仅用于构造训练奖励，不暴露给策略。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准上使用 Qwen3.5-2B，SVR 取得 0.563 的宏平均准确率，平均仅需 2.99 推理轮次。在完整系统对比中，优于标准 GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的 Oracle 引导分数反馈基线，且所需轮次远少于固定十轮推理。

Conclusion: 实验表明，学习到的自我验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，无需外部反馈即可实现高效的多轮推理。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [LLM-Based Generative Retrieval for Snapchat Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28895)
*Liam Collins,Jiwen Ren,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Xuan Guo,Mo Li,Alvin Hou,Yi Cui,Peng Yang,Jian Wang,Saud Afzal Shafi,Nga Than,Ruiming Lu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Dongheng Li,Lili Zhang,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Vincent Xue,Chunhui Zhu,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Snap推出基于LLM的生成式检索系统SnapLGR，通过语义标识符构建、持续预训练落地词表、TensorRT-LLM加速推理等联合设计，在短视频推荐A/B测试中显著提升核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM虽具备丰富语义先验和序列建模能力，但直接用作生成式检索面临两大挑战：需学习预训练中缺失的内部物品词表，且需在严格延迟和成本约束下生成合法物品标识符。

Method: 三大核心设计：1) 从多模态物品嵌入构建语义标识符(SID)，引入基于PPR的协同交互对比学习，提升码本利用率、降低冲突并注入协同信号；2) 采用持续预训练(CPT)落地SID标记，随后在用户交互序列上进行监督微调(SFT)；3) 部署层面使用TensorRT-LLM CUDA加速的束搜索及去中心化Worker-loop架构满足生产级延迟与吞吐要求。

Result: 线上A/B测试相对TIGER风格基线：观看时长+0.37%，停留时长+0.09%，深度会话+0.18%，深度会话去重用户+0.11%。离线固定分词器消融实验量化了模型架构、规模扩展和预训练各自贡献。

Conclusion: 成功落地生产级SnapLGR需要在表征学习、词表落地、高效训练与推理三方面进行联合系统设计。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) are promising retrieval engines because they combine rich semantic priors, strong sequence modeling capabilities, and favorable scaling behavior. However, turning a pretrained LLM into a generative retriever in production deployment raises several challenges: the model must learn an internal item vocabulary that was absent from pretraining, and generate valid item identifiers under strict latency and cost constraints. We address these challenges through the design and launch of SnapLGR, an LLM-based generative retrieval system for short-video recommendation at Snapchat. The system is built around three main designs. First, we construct semantic identifiers (SIDs) from multimodal item embeddings and enhance them with Personalized PageRank (PPR)-based co-engagement contrastive learning, resulting in improved codebook utilization, reduced collisions, and infused collaborative signal. Second, we use continued pretraining (CPT) to ground the introduced SID tokens before supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on user interaction sequences. Third, we make SnapLGR serving practical through TensorRT-LLM CUDA-backed beam search and a decentralized worker-loop architecture. In a live A/B test, the launched system increased View Time by 0.37%, Time Spent by 0.09%, Deep Sessions by 0.18%, and Deep Sessions Unique User by 0.11% relative to the existing TIGER-style generative retrieval baseline. We then decompose this offline gap under a fixed tokenizer and quantify the gains due to model architecture, scaling, and pretraining. Overall, our deployment shows that successful production SnapLGR requires joint design across representation learning, vocabulary grounding, and efficient training and serving.

</details>


### [91] [TransX: Scaling Transformer-based Recommendation via Behavioral and Serving Stream Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28940)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransX 是一个面向生产环境的编码器-解码器架构，将推荐重构为序列到序列的动作转导问题，显式解耦行为流建模与服务事件建模，并结合增量编码与键值缓存的分摊服务策略，在 LinkedIn 大规模推荐系统上实现 CTR 提升 6.0%、转化率提升 4.4%，同时将在线计算量降低约 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的序列推荐模型将长期用户行为与实时服务事件等异构数据源压缩为单一单片化 token 流，掩盖了其不同的因果角色与时间特性，导致建模效率低下、训练与服务成本高昂。

Method: 提出 TransX 编码器-解码器架构：1) 将推荐建模为序列到序列动作转导，显式解耦行为流编码器与服务事件解码器；2) 利用可扩展交叉注意力机制，在近线行为编码与实时服务表示间建模条件依赖；3) 协同设计分摊服务策略，结合增量行为编码与逐请求键值缓存，使服务延迟对行为序列长度不敏感。

Result: 离线实验与大规模在线 A/B 测试表明，TransX 持续超越最先进的深度学习推荐模型（DLRM）与序列基线，CTR 提升 6.0%，转化率提升 4.4%，服务成本与现有生产模型相当，协同服务策略将在线计算量减少约 80%。

Conclusion: TransX 通过显式解耦异构数据建模与协同服务优化，有效解决了工业级推荐系统中长期行为与实时事件融合的效率与性能瓶颈，实现了显著的业务指标提升与极低的服务开销，具备生产落地价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems (RecSys) increasingly adopt Transformer-based sequence models, with an emerging paradigm that frames recommendation as next-token prediction over a unified monolithic user sequence. However, collapsing heterogeneous data sources -- such as long-term user behaviors and real-time serving events -- into a single monolithic token stream that obscures their distinct causal roles and temporal characteristics, leading to inefficient modeling and elevated training and serving costs. We propose TransX, a production-oriented encoder-decoder architecture that reformulates recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence action transduction problem. TransX explicitly decouples behavior-stream modeling from serving-event modeling and conditions next-action decoding on scalable cross-attention between nearline behavior encodings and real-time serving representations. To enable low-latency, high-QPS deployment, TransX is co-designed with an amortized serving strategy that combines incremental behavior encoding with per-request key-value caching, rendering serving latency insensitive to behavior sequence length. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests on LinkedIn's recommender systems show that TransX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DLRMs and sequential baselines, and delivers substantial CTR lift (+6.0%) and conversion gain (+4.4%) while maintaining serving costs comparable to existing production models where our co-designed serving strategy reduces online computation by approximately 80%.

</details>


### [92] [GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29213)
*Jiping Liu,Zhongmin Zhang,Zisen Sang,Zhijia Fang,Tao Ouyang,Ma Jiang,Shaopeng Liang,Zeyang Hou,Guodong Cao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GALA 是一种三阶段多模态推荐流水线，通过行为感知三元组预训练、生成式 RL 对齐（GRPO）和自适应门控融合，解决了内容语义预训练与行为驱动排序模型分离导致的对齐难题，在淘宝闪购大规模部署并显著提升订单量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品外卖推荐系统虽利用图像、文本、交互历史等多模态信号，但异构模态融合困难，且主流两阶段方法将内容语义预训练与行为驱动排序模型割裂，导致语义理解与用户行为模式难以对齐，无法有效捕捉动态用户意图。

Method: GALA 包含三阶段：1) 行为感知三元组预训练，利用搜索日志中的查询-图像-文本三元组早期捕获用户意图与内容偏好；2) 生成式 RL 对齐中间阶段，基于转换奖励的 GRPO 优化多模态嵌入，动态对齐用户行为并弥合预训练-微调鸿沟；3) 自适应门控融合多模态与 ID 嵌入，配合混合损失函数，在长期 ID 主导训练中保留多模态贡献。

Result: 已在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务 2 亿+ 日活用户。离线实验相比 SOTA 方法 AUC 提升 +0.12/+0.20，PCOC 指标更优；大规模在线 A/B 测试订单量提升 0.55%，验证了工业级有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GALA 通过创新的中间生成式 RL 对齐阶段，成功弥合预训练与微调的鸿沟，实现多模态语义与用户行为的深度对齐，在超大规模工业场景中显著提升推荐效果，证明了其在复杂需求模式下的实用价值。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems in food delivery increasingly leverage multimodal signals, including images, text, and user interaction histories, to enhance user experience, yet effective fusion of these heterogeneous modalities remains challenging, hindering both the joint modeling of multimodal signals and adaptation to evolving user intent. In mainstream two-stage approaches, the separation between content-semantic pretraining of image-text encoders and behavior-driven ranking models limits alignment between semantic understanding and user behavior patterns. To address these issues, we present GALA, a three-stage pipeline whose core innovation lies in an intermediate "generative RL alignment" stage that constructs multimodal pretraining data from user behavior and refines it via conversion-based rewards, effectively bridging the pretraining-fine-tuning gap to align with downstream objectives. GALA comprises three stages: first, behavior-aware triplet pretraining on query-image-text pairs from search logs to early capture user intent and content preferences; second, a novel intermediate stage that refines multimodal embeddings through reward-driven optimization (GRPO) to dynamically align them with user behavior and bridge the pretraining-fine-tuning gap; and finally, integration of multimodal and ID embeddings via adaptive gating with a hybrid loss, preserving multimodal contributions under long-term ID-dominant training. GALA has been deployed in the production environment at Taobao Shangou, serving over 200 million daily active users. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, it delivers consistent offline gains of +0.12/+0.20 AUC along with better PCOC metrics. Large-scale online A/B tests further report a 0.55 percent increase in order volume, confirming GALA's effectiveness at industrial scale and its robustness across diverse demand patterns.

</details>


### [93] [Language Models Agree With Each Other, Not With Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29274)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文通过自然人类标注数据（2,523个读者在120篇网页文档上的高亮标记）而非人工众包标注，测量语言模型之间的趋同/同质化程度。结果显示：模型间一致性显著高于人类间一致性（中位数+0.093 vs +0.040），两个竞争实验室的前沿模型达+0.203，是GPT-4o自我一致性的两倍；最小模型仅达人类水平，无模型与读者的一致性超过读者间一致性。该效应呈梯度分布，非由确定性、提示词、流程、厂商或路由导致。事后测试4个新发布模型均未超出人类区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于语言模型同质化的声明通常基于为研究专门收集的人类判断，使得人类侧成为设计伪影：给予模型指令的众包工作者本质上是在运行模型的提示词。作者旨在针对一个无人为研究目的构建的自然人类参考系——人们出于自身原因在默认关闭他人标记叠加的平台上高亮文档——来测量模型收敛程度。

Method: 使用某平台上2,523组读者标记集，跨越120篇网页文档（默认关闭他人标记叠加）。一致性定义为两个等规模句子集的重叠度，减去各自在深度和长度分桶内重采样的期望重叠度。空假设校准通过演示而非断言：所有涉及随机基线的配对均落在零点±0.006内。测试18个模型臂，覆盖11家厂商、3个国家及两种权重制度，共153个模型配对。

Result: 中位文档各方标记14/70句；两读者共享4.1句，两模型共享8.7句。153个模型配对中位一致性+0.093，人类基准+0.040，99个配对完全高于人类区间。两家竞争实验室的前沿模型达+0.203，为GPT-4o二次调用自我一致性的两倍。效应呈梯度：最小模型处于人类水平。无模型与读者一致性显著超越读者间一致性；在等深度和长度下，无表层特征能区分其选择。钝化模型输出可减半差距但无法消除。4个事后发布的模型均未超出人类区间。

Conclusion: 语言模型表现出显著的同质化趋同，形成单一而非多重分布。模型间高度一致性非程序伪影，且随模型规模梯度增强。模拟自多个模型的总体并非多个总体，而是单一总体。

Abstract: Claims that language models homogenise are usually measured against human judgements collected for the study, which makes the human side an artifact of the design: a crowdworker given the model's instruction is running the model's prompt. We measure convergence against a human reference nobody built for the purpose -- 2,523 reader mark sets across 120 web documents, produced by people highlighting for their own reasons on a platform where the overlay of others' marks is off by default.
  Agreement is the overlap between two size-matched sentence sets minus the overlap expected when each is resampled within its own depth-and-length bands. The null's calibration is demonstrated, not asserted: every pair involving a random baseline lands within 0.006 of zero. On the median document each party names 14 sentences of 70; two readers share 4.1 and two models 8.7.
  Across 18 model arms spanning 11 vendors, 3 countries and both weight regimes, the median of 153 model pairs is +0.093 against a human yardstick of +0.040, and 99 sit entirely above the human interval. Two frontier models from rival labs reach +0.203, twice what GPT-4o agrees with itself on a second call. The effect is not determinism, prompt wording, procedure, vendor or routing, and it is graded: the smallest models agree at the human level. No model agrees with readers detectably more than a reader does, and at equal depth and length no surface feature separates their choices.
  The multiples are procedure-dependent and the ordering is not: models are cut to their sharpest set while a reader's is a random draw from what they marked, and blunting the models alike halves the gap without closing it. Tested out of sample on four models released after this analysis, against predictions fixed beforehand, none clears the human interval. A population simulated from several models is not several populations.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging the Question-Answer Gap in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29402)
*Domen Vake,Jernej Vičič,Aleksandar Tošić*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE框架，将假设性内容生成从查询阶段移至索引阶段，通过预计算多个假设提示并嵌入文档块，将检索转化为问题-问题匹配，提升检索精度和召回，无额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中用户查询与文档文本存在风格差距，现有运行时方案（如HyDE）虽改善对齐但引入查询时计算开销，需解决此问题。

Method: HyPE框架在索引阶段为每个数据块预计算多个假设提示，将文档块嵌入替代提示嵌入，将检索转化为问题-问题匹配任务，避免运行时合成答案生成。

Result: 在六个常用数据集上，HyPE将检索上下文精度提升高达42个百分点，声明召回率提升高达45个百分点，且兼容重排序、多向量检索、查询分解等RAG进阶技术。

Conclusion: HyPE通过将假设内容生成前置至索引阶段，有效弥合查询与文档风格差距，显著提升检索性能且不增加延迟，为RAG系统提供了一种高效且兼容的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems synergize retrieval mechanisms with generative language models to enhance the accuracy and relevance of responses. However, bridging the style gap between user queries and relevant information in document text remains a persistent challenge in retrieval-augmented systems, often addressed by runtime solutions (e.g., Hypothetical Document Embeddings (HyDE)) that attempt to improve alignment but introduce extra computational overhead at query time. To address these challenges, we propose Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings (HyPE), a framework that shifts the generation of hypothetical content from query time to the indexing phase. By precomputing multiple hypothetical prompts for each data chunk and embedding the chunk in place of the prompt, HyPE transforms retrieval into a question-question matching task, bypassing the need for runtime synthetic answer generation. This approach does not introduce latency but also strengthens the alignment between queries and relevant context. Our experimental results on six common datasets show that HyPE can improve retrieval context precision by up to 42 percentage points and claim recall by up to 45 percentage points, compared to standard approaches, while remaining compatible with re-ranking, multi-vector retrieval, query decomposition, and other RAG advancements

</details>


### [95] [QASP: Query-Adaptive Robust Vector Search Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29606)
*Hakan Ferhatosmanoglu,Kushal Kumar,Tal Wagner,Andy Warfield*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: QASP是一种查询自适应的向量搜索策略，通过一次回归预测每查询的召回率曲线，实现对任意召回目标的自适应搜索，大幅降低召回率方差和数据访问量，且泛化能力强，理论有保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量搜索的基本挑战是在最小化计算成本的同时实现一致的高召回率。固定搜索参数导致跨查询性能差异大，传统平均召回率评估掩盖了逐查询差异。

Method: 引入QASP，通过单次预先监督回归预测每个查询的完整召回率进展曲线，为任意召回目标推导搜索策略，避免搜索时迭代模型调用或每目标单独预测器。利用归一化召回率值、尺度不变特征和搜索前推理实现跨目标、索引配置、数据集泛化；细粒度进展预测支持轻量级反应式补丁，根据预测与观测偏差调整搜索深度，无需额外推理。

Result: 实验显示QASP显著降低召回率方差和偏离目标值，提高查询满足率，能扩展到大规模数据和分层索引无需重新训练，在99%召回率下数据访问减少80%。

Conclusion: 理论证明QASP所需训练样本有限且与数据集大小、维度无关；其损失超过任何固定策略不可约下界仅消失边际；数据访问节省相对于固定探测随内在维度指数增长。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge of vector search is achieving consistently high recall while minimizing computational costs. Fixed search parameters cause significant performance variance across queries, and conventional evaluation on average recall masks these per-query disparities. We introduce QASP (Query-Adaptive robust vector Search Policy), which predicts the complete recall progression curve per query via a single upfront supervised regression, from which a search policy is derived for any recall target; this avoids iterative model invocations during search or separate predictors per target. By predicting normalized recall values with scale-invariant features and pre-search inference, QASP generalizes across recall targets, index configurations, and datasets. Its fine-grained progress predictions further enable a lightweight reactive complement that adjusts search depth based on predicted-versus-observed deviations without additional inference. We prove that QASP requires a finite training sample independent of dataset size and dimensionality, that its loss exceeds the irreducible lower bound of any fixed policy by a vanishing margin, and that its data access savings over fixed probing grow exponentially in intrinsic dimensionality. Experimentally, QASP achieves significantly lower recall variance and deviation from target, higher query satisfaction rate, and scales to large data and hierarchical indices without retraining, achieving 99% recall with 80% less data access.

</details>


### [96] [RecHarness: A Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29241)
*Haoran Ling,Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Shuwen Kang,Chi Lu,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 RecHarness，一种基于多臂老虎机路由的智能体框架，用于自动化推荐模型优化。该框架将优化过程分为两步：老虎机路由器根据历史验证反馈选择修改方向，LLM 在选定方向内生成具体优化假设及可执行代码修改。引入跳盆机制在局部编辑停滞时触发结构性跳跃，实现长时程探索。实验表明 RecHarness 在多任务、多数据集、多骨干网络上比纯 LLM 推理搜索更稳定、更高效地利用有限试验预算；在大规模短视频广告平台 7 天线上 A/B 测试中，ADVV 提升 2.084%，收入提升 0.534%，曝光提升 0.559%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型优化仍严重依赖工程师手动迭代架构、目标函数和训练策略。虽然基于 LLM 的智能体可自动化这一试错过程，但让 LLM 同时决定修改方向并生成具体假设往往导致在有限实验预算下搜索不稳定。因此需要一种能分离方向选择与假设生成、并支持长时程探索的自动化优化框架。

Method: RecHarness 采用两步解耦设计：1) Bandit Router（老虎机路由器）根据历史验证反馈，从预定义修改方向（如架构、损失函数、训练策略等）中选择下一步探索方向；2) LLM Agent 在选定方向内生成具体优化假设及可执行代码编辑。为避免局部最优，引入 Jump-Basin 机制：当局部编辑连续停滞时，激活结构性跳跃臂，进行大幅度架构或策略变更。整个过程在有限试验预算下闭环迭代。

Result: 在多个推荐任务（点击率预测、序列推荐等）、多个公开数据集及多种主流骨干网络（DIN, SASRec 等）上，RecHarness 相比纯 LLM 推理搜索、随机搜索等基线，能更稳定地获得性能提升，且在相同试验预算下收敛更快。线上 A/B 测试（大规模短视频广告平台，7 天）显示：ADVV +2.084%，Revenue +0.534%，Exposure +0.559%。

Conclusion: RecHarness 通过老虎机路由分离方向选择与假设生成，结合跳盆机制实现长时程探索，显著提升了自动化推荐模型优化的稳定性和样本效率，并在工业级线上环境中验证了实际业务收益。代码已开源，为后续推荐系统自动化优化研究提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Optimizing modern recommender models still depends heavily on engineers manually iterating over architectural, objective, and training-strategy changes. While LLM-based agents can automate this trial-and-error process, allowing the LLM to both select modification directions and generate concrete hypotheses often leads to unstable search under limited experiment budgets. Inspired by the above challenge, we propose RecHarness, a Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for automated recommender model optimization. RecHarness separates the optimization process into two steps: a bandit router selects the next modification direction according to historical validation feedback, while the LLM generates a concrete optimization hypothesis and executable code edit within the selected direction. To sustain long-horizon exploration, RecHarness uses a jump-basin mechanism to activate a structural-jump arm when local edits stagnate. Across multiple recommendation tasks, datasets, and model backbones, RecHarness achieves more stable performance improvements and uses limited trial budgets more effectively than LLM-reasoning search. During a 7-day online A/B test on a large-scale short-video advertising platform, the selected candidate improves ADVV by 2.084%, Revenue by 0.534%, and Exposure by 0.559%. Code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecHarness.

</details>


### [97] [RareSense: Rarity-Aware Similarity Search for Anomaly Retrieval in Transactional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28879)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RareSense框架，通过挖掘极小稀有项集和稀有关联规则，将稀疏集值数据映射为稀有规则画像，利用加权Jaccard相似度进行相似性搜索，解决传统原子级重叠度量被频繁背景属性主导的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相似度量（Jaccard、余弦、汉明）基于原子级特征重叠，导致稀疏集值数据的相似性搜索被高频背景属性主导；IDF加权虽能部分缓解但仍局限于单特征，无法显式捕捉信息量大的高阶共现模式。

Method: 1) 挖掘极小稀有项集作为中间结构；2) 推导可靠的稀有关联规则；3) 将对象映射为稀疏稀有规则画像；4) 基于反向支持度、置信度、提升度、结构复杂度和稳定性组合的规则权重，使用加权Jaccard相似度比较画像。

Result: 在跨网络安全和一般分类领域的四个基准族实验中，RareSense在宏平均查询条件检索性能上优于所有原子基线，统计检验显示显著差异；全局异常排序任务中也达到最高宏平均性能，且与强专用检测器统计可比。增益随工作负载变化，在异常共享可复现稀有高阶结构时最强。

Conclusion: RareSense将IDF加权Jaccard推广为稀有规则层面的框架，诱导距离在原始对象上为伪度量、在相同规则画像等价类上为度量；实证证明其在稀疏事务异常数据的相似性搜索和异常检测中显著优于原子级基线。

Abstract: Similarity search over sparse set-valued data is often dominated by frequent background attributes because classical measures such as Jaccard, cosine, and Hamming compare objects through atomic overlap. IDF (Inverse document frequency) weighting partially reduces this effect but remains atom-wise and cannot explicitly represent informative higher-order co-occurrences. We introduce RareSense, a rarity-aware similarity framework for sparse transactional anomaly data. RareSense mines minimal rare itemsets as intermediate structures, derives reliable rare association rules, maps objects into sparse rare-rule profiles, and compares them using weighted Jaccard similarity. Rule weights combine inverse support, confidence, lift, structural complexity, and stability, so that neighborhoods are determined by shared rare evidence rather than uniform feature overlap. We show that IDF-weighted Jaccard is a restricted singleton case of RareSense, and that the induced distance is a pseudometric on the original objects and a metric over equivalence classes defined by identical rule profiles. Experiments across four benchmark families spanning cybersecurity and general categorical domains show that RareSense attains the highest observed macro-average query-conditioned retrieval performance among the evaluated similarity measures. The statistical analysis indicates significant overall differences, with corrected paired comparisons favoring RareSense over the atomic baselines. The gains remain workload-dependent and are strongest when anomalies share repeatable rare higher-order structure. For global anomaly ranking, RareSense achieves the highest observed macro-average performance while remaining statistically comparable to several strong dedicated detectors.

</details>


### [98] [Don't Contrast the Impossible: Region-Constrained Batching for Contrastive User Modeling on a Local Community Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28971)
*Seungho Han,Byeongchang Kim,Jin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对本地社区平台（如Karrot）推荐系统中对比学习面临的“地理约束曝光”问题，提出区域约束批次采样（RCBS）方法，通过构建区域同质化的mini-batch，将不可能的负样本替换为可行的负样本，显著提升用户表示质量，并在线上A/B测试中验证了对主页流排序、检索及广告排序的提升效果，已部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对比学习假设全域曝光（任意用户可被展示任意物品），但本地社区平台存在地理硬约束，导致大量用户-物品对本质上不可能交互，却仍被作为负样本参与训练，稀释了对比学习信号，降低了负样本的信息量与难度。

Method: 提出区域约束批次采样（RCBS）：在构建训练mini-batch时，仅采样同一地理区域内的用户与物品，使batch内的负样本均为用户实际可能曝光的候选集，从而将不可能负样本替换为可行且更困难的负样本，无需修改模型架构或损失函数。

Result: 离线评估与在线A/B测试均显示RCBS持续提升用户嵌入质量，进而改善主页流排序、检索召回及展示广告排序指标；该用户嵌入已在多个生产环境应用中部署上线。

Conclusion: RCBS是一种简单高效的批次采样策略，通过显式建模地理曝光约束解决不可能负样本问题，在不增加计算开销前提下显著增强对比学习信号，具有良好的工程落地性与泛化性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is widely used for user modeling in large-scale recommender systems, where standard in-batch negatives implicitly assume universal exposure that any user can be shown any item. On local community platforms such as Karrot, however, exposure is geographically constrained; many user-item pairs are impossible by design yet still treated as negatives during training, diluting the contrastive learning signal. We address this impossible negatives problem and propose Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS), a simple yet effective batching method that constructs region-homogeneous mini-batches so that users are contrasted primarily against items they could feasibly see. By replacing impossible negatives with feasible ones, RCBS naturally introduces harder and more informative negatives under realistic exposure constraints. With offline evaluations and online A/B tests, we show that RCBS consistently improves user representation quality and consequently enhances home feed ranking, retrieval, and display ads ranking. The resulting user embeddings have been deployed in production across various applications.

</details>


### [99] [PaletteID: Prototype-Composed Semantic Identifiers for Multimodal CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29000)
*Huanyu Liu,Baining Chen,Hui Liu,Zengyang Li,Ziyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出PaletteID (PID)，一种基于原型的语义标识符，通过SQ-DPP构建原型调色板，利用语义相关原型聚合表示物品，有效解决CTR预测中的冷启动和长尾问题，优于现有离散化SID方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将预训练多模态嵌入离散化为语义标识符(SID)，但存在两大局限：码本分配无法保留语义相关性且丢弃细粒度连续信号；残差码路径高度依赖前缀码，限制了层级标识符的表征可扩展性。因此需要一种能保留语义细粒度信息且具有更好扩展性的标识符方法。

Method: 提出PaletteID (PID)，灵感来自调色板颜色组合。首先利用语义质量感知确定性点过程(SQ-DPP)构建原型调色板，兼顾局部内容密度和全局语义多样性。然后为每个目标物品检索语义相关的原型序列，并将其聚合为信息丰富的PID表示，实现丰富互补的语义建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上进行大量实验，结果表明PID持续提升CTR预测性能，且对长尾物品增益更大。PID还产生更稳健的标识符分配，并提供比现有残差SID方法更可解释的token语义。

Conclusion: PaletteID通过基于原型的语义标识符有效桥接预训练多模态内容空间与推荐模型，解决了现有离散化SID方法的局限性，在CTR预测、冷启动和长尾问题上表现优异，且具有更好的可解释性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal information can improve the accuracy of click-through rate (CTR) prediction and effectively alleviate item cold-start and long-tail problems. Recent studies commonly discretize pretrained multimodal embeddings into semantic identifiers (SIDs), allowing the model to learn task-specific semantic representations for recommendation. However, existing methods still provide limited gains due to two major limitations. First, codebook assignment fails to preserve semantic relevance and discards fine-grained continuous signals in the original embedding space. Second, the residual code paths are highly dependent on prefix codes, which limits the effective representational scalability of hierarchical identifiers. To address these issues, we propose PaletteID (PID), a prototype-based semantic identifier. Inspired by palette-based color composition, PID uses a compact set of representative prototype items as semantic anchors to bridge pretrained multimodal content space and recommendation models. Specifically, we first construct a prototype palette with Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process (SQ-DPP), which jointly considers local content density and global semantic diversity. Then, for each target item, PID retrieves a sequence of semantically related prototypes and aggregates them into an informative PID representation, enabling rich and complementary semantic modeling. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that PID consistently improves CTR prediction and yields larger gains for long-tail items. PID also produces more robust identifier assignments and provides more interpretable token semantics than existing residual SID methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LAWFUL框架，解决神经网络学习物理定律时的可解释性缺口，并在Mocap2Radar Transformer上验证其能内化并使用多普勒频率定律 f(t)=2v(t)/λ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在准确预测物理系统时，是否真正学习了作为形式化结构知识的物理定律，且其内部计算是否在定律适用域内实际使用该表征。针对连续变量物理定律，现有方法存在四大可解释性缺口：缺乏覆盖感知的因果一致性度量、已识别电路的适用域测试、定律不变量与禁令行为的验证、以及物理量在电路中流动的量化。

Method: 构建基础框架LAWFUL，封闭前两个缺口（因果一致性度量与适用域测试），为后两个缺口奠定基础。以Mocap2Radar Transformer为案例，利用动作捕捉与雷达数据（均不显式包含 f(t) 或 v(t)），验证网络是否学习并内部使用多普勒频率定律 f(t)=2v(t)/λ。

Result: 实验表明，Mocap2Radar Transformer 成功学习了多普勒频率定律，且其内部计算在定律适用域内始终沿用该表征，验证了LAWFUL框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LAWFUL框架为连续变量物理定律在神经网络中的可解释性提供了系统性工具，能够量化因果一致性、测试适用域，并为不变量验证与物理量流动分析铺平道路，推动了物理知神经网络的可信解释。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [101] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEDR-Seq2P，一种轻量级Seq2Point扩展，用于工业非侵入式负载监测（NILM），在保持精度的同时显著降低推理延迟，适合大规模工业部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业NILM面临测量噪声和并发机器操作导致的模型泛化性差问题，现有模型在精度与延迟之间难以平衡，需要轻量级高精度模型。

Method: 采用一对多多任务分解设置，基准测试Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet；提出SEDR-Seq2P，结合膨胀残差块和挤压激励注意力机制的轻量级Seq2Point扩展。

Result: SEDR-Seq2P相对Seq2Point基线MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%；相比WaveNet推理延迟降低约58%，实现良好的精度-延迟权衡。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P为工业NILM提供了一种可扩展的轻量级解决方案，在精度与计算效率间取得优异平衡，适合实际工业部署。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [102] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为CV的计算机视觉框架，直接从光学显微照片预测轻量化合金钢的疲劳寿命（log N_f），结合物理信息特征提取和基于高斯负对数似然损失的CNN回归模型，在合成显微照片基准上实现高精度预测（R²=0.93）和优秀的不确定性校准（ECE降低76%），推理速度<65ms，代码和数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疲劳寿命评估需要持续数十至数百小时的机械试验，难以满足快速质量控制的需求，因此需要一种无需物理测试、直接从显微图像快速预测疲劳寿命的方法。

Method: 构建CV框架：1) 七阶段OpenCV预处理流程去除伪影；2) 28维物理信息特征提取器，量化裂纹形貌、晶粒结构、孔隙率和纹理；3) 采用高斯负对数似然（GNLL）损失训练CNN回归模型，联合预测log N_f和样本特定不确定性σ̂；4) 在合成显微照片基准上评估SE-CNN、ResNet-50、VGG-16三种架构。

Result: ResNet-50达到R²=0.93、RMSE=0.18 log-cycles、macro-F1=0.91；GNLL目标使期望校准误差（ECE）较MSE基线降低76%（0.089→0.021）；Grad-CAM热力图证实网络关注冶金学意义的微观结构特征；单图推理耗时<65ms；管道及合成数据集生成器已开源。

Conclusion: 结果基于合成显微照片验证，证明了方法在模拟条件下的合理性；下一步需开展真实现场样本的迁移学习研究。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [103] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CALCoDe（类尾部自适应局部一致性延迟），一种用于冻结医学视觉语言模型的后处理可靠性层，解决临床分布偏移下单个疾病类别覆盖率不足的问题，通过交叉拟合验证识别风险类别、估计类条件尾部阈值并结合局部一致性阈值，实现有限样本覆盖保证。在两个皮肤病学分布偏移实验和四个VLM骨干网络上，CALCoDe是唯一在所有八种设置下边际覆盖率和最差类别接受覆盖率均达到0.95的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在临床分布偏移后虽然能保持较高的观测边际覆盖率，但会严重低估单个疾病类别的覆盖率。受影响类别随采集协议和骨干网络几何结构变化，源域类别频率无法可靠揭示失效。现有的局部一致性方法和尾部感知一致性方法分别适应测试邻域和源域频率尾部，但未建模留出类别级覆盖率失效。

Method: CALCoDe包含三个核心组件：1) 交叉拟合验证预测识别欠覆盖风险类别；2) 不相交校准分割估计类条件尾部阈值，并通过单侧最大值与局部一致性阈值结合；3) 独立校准的支持审计延迟内点支持不足的案例。在接受示例内可交换性假设下，提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在HAM10000到ISIC 2019和HAM10000到PAD-UFES-20两个皮肤病学分布偏移上，针对BiomedCLIP、OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32、PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32、MedSigLIP-448四个冻结VLM骨干，CALCoDe是唯一在所有八种设置下观测边际覆盖率和最差类别接受覆盖率均达到0.95的方法。HAM10000到ISIC 2019上平均最差类别接受覆盖率为0.970，优于sTACP的0.926和LCP-VLM的0.864。

Conclusion: CALCoDe通过类条件尾部自适应和局部一致性结合，有效解决了医学VLM在分布偏移下的类别级覆盖率失效问题，提供理论保证的有限样本覆盖率，并在多个真实临床分布偏移场景中显著优于现有最先进的一致性预测基线方法。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>
